--- Fichier : \google52f8545b5796aa46.html ---

--- Fichier : \grid-gaps.js ---

/* =====================================================================
   grid-gaps.js
   Coupe les pointillÃ©s VERTICAUX (fixes) Ã  hauteur de chaque cadre "+",
   pour qu'ils n'entrent pas dans la zone encadrÃ©e.
   Principe : un masque CSS dÃ©gradÃ© est recalculÃ© au scroll pour laisser
   un trou transparent autour de chaque croix visible.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
    'use strict';

    var GAP = 16;        // demi-hauteur de la coupure (px) ; cohÃ©rent avec --cross-gap
    var Y_OFFSET = 1;    // le cadre est ~1px plus bas que le centre de la croix
    var ticking = false;

    function build() {
        ticking = false;

        var lines = document.querySelectorAll('.grid-line-vertical');
        if (!lines.length) return;

        var h = window.innerHeight;
        var centers = [];

        document.querySelectorAll('.grid-divider .grid-cross.left').forEach(function (c) {
            var r = c.getBoundingClientRect();
            var y = r.top + r.height / 2 + Y_OFFSET;
            if (y > -GAP * 2 && y < h + GAP * 2) centers.push(y);
        });
        centers.sort(function (a, b) { return a - b; });

        var stops = ['#000 0'];
        centers.forEach(function (y) {
            stops.push(
                '#000 ' + (y - GAP) + 'px',
                'transparent ' + (y - GAP) + 'px',
                'transparent ' + (y + GAP) + 'px',
                '#000 ' + (y + GAP) + 'px'
            );
        });
        var mask = 'linear-gradient(to bottom, ' + stops.join(', ') + ')';

        lines.forEach(function (l) {
            l.style.webkitMaskImage = mask;
            l.style.maskImage = mask;
        });
    }

    function request() {
        if (!ticking) {
            ticking = true;
            window.requestAnimationFrame(build);
        }
    }

    window.addEventListener('scroll', request, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener('resize', request);
    window.addEventListener('load', request);
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', request);
    request();
})();


--- Fichier : \grid-style.css ---

/* =====================================================================
   grid-style.css  -  nouveau style des dÃ©limiteurs (le "+" encadrÃ©)
   - le "+" n'est plus nÃ©on (plus de bleu fluo ni de lueur)
   - les pointillÃ©s ne pÃ©nÃ¨trent plus dans la zone encadrÃ©e :
       . horizontaux : coupÃ©s autour du cadre (masque CSS ci-dessous)
       . verticaux   : coupÃ©s autour de chaque cadre par grid-gaps.js
   Ã€ charger APRÃˆS style.css (index.html et skills.html).
   RÃ©glages : --cross-color (couleur du +), --cross-gap (demi-ouverture
   de la coupure, en px).
   ===================================================================== */

:root {
    --cross-color: #b9c8d3;   /* gris-bleu clair, sobre */
    --cross-gap: 16px;        /* demi-largeur de la coupure autour du cadre */
}

/* --- Le "+" : sobre, sans lueur --- */
.grid-cross {
    color: var(--cross-color);
    text-shadow: none;
    font-weight: 300;
}

/* --- PointillÃ©s horizontaux : coupÃ©s autour du cadre --- */
/* ligne de gauche : le cadre est centrÃ© sur la ligne verticale gauche */
.grid-cross.left + .grid-line-horizontal {
    -webkit-mask-image: linear-gradient(to right,
        #000 0,
        #000 calc(var(--grid-margin) - var(--cross-gap)),
        transparent calc(var(--grid-margin) - var(--cross-gap)),
        transparent calc(var(--grid-margin) + var(--cross-gap)),
        #000 calc(var(--grid-margin) + var(--cross-gap)));
    mask-image: linear-gradient(to right,
        #000 0,
        #000 calc(var(--grid-margin) - var(--cross-gap)),
        transparent calc(var(--grid-margin) - var(--cross-gap)),
        transparent calc(var(--grid-margin) + var(--cross-gap)),
        #000 calc(var(--grid-margin) + var(--cross-gap)));
}
/* ligne de droite : cadre centrÃ© sur la ligne verticale droite (mesurÃ© depuis le bord droit) */
.grid-title + .grid-line-horizontal {
    -webkit-mask-image: linear-gradient(to left,
        #000 0,
        #000 calc(var(--grid-margin) - var(--cross-gap) + 2px),
        transparent calc(var(--grid-margin) - var(--cross-gap) + 2px),
        transparent calc(var(--grid-margin) + var(--cross-gap) + 2px),
        #000 calc(var(--grid-margin) + var(--cross-gap) + 2px));
    mask-image: linear-gradient(to left,
        #000 0,
        #000 calc(var(--grid-margin) - var(--cross-gap) + 2px),
        transparent calc(var(--grid-margin) - var(--cross-gap) + 2px),
        transparent calc(var(--grid-margin) + var(--cross-gap) + 2px),
        #000 calc(var(--grid-margin) + var(--cross-gap) + 2px));
}

    /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
       LANGUE PAR DÃ‰FAUT = ANGLAIS
       ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
    let stored = localStorage.getItem('lang');
    let currentLang = (stored === 'fr' || stored === 'en') ? stored : 'en';

    function syncLoaderMessages(lang) {
        if (!translations[lang] || !translations[lang]['loader.messages']) return;
        window.__loaderMessages = window.__loaderMessages || [];
        const msgs = translations[lang]['loader.messages'];
        window.__loaderMessages.length = 0;
        msgs.forEach(m => window.__loaderMessages.push(m));
    }

    function applyLanguage(lang) {
        if (!translations[lang]) return;
        currentLang = lang;
        localStorage.setItem('lang', lang);
        document.documentElement.lang = lang;

        const dict = translations[lang];

        // 1) textContent (pour les labels visibles : .hud-label, nav, sous-titresâ€¦)
        document.querySelectorAll('[data-i18n]').forEach(el => {
            const key = el.getAttribute('data-i18n');
            if (key && dict[key] !== undefined) el.textContent = dict[key];
        });

        // 2) innerHTML (paragraphes avec <span>/<strong>/<br>â€¦)
        document.querySelectorAll('[data-i18n-html]').forEach(el => {
            const key = el.getAttribute('data-i18n-html');
            if (key && dict[key] !== undefined) el.innerHTML = dict[key];
        });

        // 3) HUD cards : attribut data-title (utilisÃ© par main.js au clic)
        document.querySelectorAll('[data-i18n-title]').forEach(el => {
            const key = el.getAttribute('data-i18n-title');
            if (key && dict[key] !== undefined) el.setAttribute('data-title', dict[key]);
        });

        // 4) HUD cards : attribut data-desc (utilisÃ© par main.js au clic)
        document.querySelectorAll('[data-i18n-desc]').forEach(el => {
            const key = el.getAttribute('data-i18n-desc');
            if (key && dict[key] !== undefined) el.setAttribute('data-desc', dict[key]);
        });

        // 5) Compat : attributs gÃ©nÃ©riques "attr1:key1,attr2:key2"
        document.querySelectorAll('[data-i18n-attr]').forEach(el => {
            const spec = el.getAttribute('data-i18n-attr');
            if (!spec) return;
            spec.split(',').forEach(pair => {
                const parts = pair.split(':');
                if (parts.length < 2) return;
                const attr = parts[0].trim();
                const key  = parts.slice(1).join(':').trim();
                if (attr && key && dict[key] !== undefined) {
                    el.setAttribute(attr, dict[key]);
                }
            });
        });

        // 6) Si un projet est actuellement affichÃ©, on met Ã  jour le popup en direct
        const activeCard = document.querySelector('.hud-card.active');
        const screenTitle = document.getElementById('screen-title');
        const screenDesc  = document.getElementById('screen-desc');
        if (activeCard && screenTitle && activeCard.classList.contains('active')) {
            // On ne rÃ©Ã©crit que si le popup est ouvert (display != none)
            const layer = document.getElementById('tactical-content-layer');
            if (layer && layer.style.display !== 'none') {
                const t = activeCard.getAttribute('data-title');
                const d = activeCard.getAttribute('data-desc');
                if (t) screenTitle.innerText = t;
                if (d && screenDesc) screenDesc.innerHTML = d;
            }
        }

        syncLoaderMessages(lang);

        document.querySelectorAll('.btn-lang').forEach(btn => {
            btn.classList.toggle('active', btn.dataset.lang === lang);
        });
    }

    window.setLanguage = applyLanguage;
    window.getLanguage = () => currentLang;

    syncLoaderMessages(currentLang);

    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
        document.querySelectorAll('.btn-lang').forEach(btn => {
            btn.addEventListener('click', () => applyLanguage(btn.dataset.lang));
        });
        applyLanguage(currentLang);
    });
})();

                <p class="file-desc" data-i18n-html="intern.1.desc">
                    During this internship at the <strong>naval air base</strong> in HyÃ¨res, the centre entrusted me with the task of developing an <strong>innovative module</strong> for the <strong>French Navy</strong>. My objective was to design a <strong>testing infrastructure</strong>.
                    <br><br>
                    This design allowed me to develop my knowledge of <strong>3D modelling</strong> and combine the expertise of machinists and engineers in multidisciplinary fields. This experience was decisive, consolidating my focus on <strong>defence systems engineering</strong> and <strong>military aeronautics</strong>.

            <div class="file-data">
                <div class="file-header"><span class="file-id">CRTA Zagreb // 2026</span></div>
                <h3 class="file-title" data-i18n="intern.2.title">Robotic and Drone Sectors R&D</h3>
                <!-- Le contenu est entiÃ¨rement remplacÃ© par i18n (le lien GitHub est dans la chaÃ®ne de traduction) -->
                <p class="file-desc" data-i18n-html="intern.2.desc">
                    My role was to implement a <strong>Visual-Inertial Odometry</strong> system on a drone and to perform <strong>algorithm benchmarking for fusion</strong>. A platform, comprising a Jetson mini-computer and sensors, retrieves topics via a ROS2 architecture for <strong>EKF, UKF, and VIO</strong> pose estimation algorithms.
                    <br><br>
                    This project allowed me to gain <strong>hands-on experience</strong> with tuning sensor fusion algorithms. Configure these algorithms is learned through empirical testing and not with courses. I was also able to familiarize myself with <strong>embedded systems for drones</strong>. You can find the project on GitHub:
                    <br>
                    <a href="https://github.com/timm-clv/SensorFusion/tree/main" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style="color: #E13C4C; text-decoration: underline; font-weight: bold;">
                        https://github.com/timm-clv/SensorFusion/tree/main
                    </a>
                </p>

/* =====================================================================
   FILETS DE SÃ‰CURITÃ‰ MOBILE (ex-mobile-safety.js)
   Garantit que la page ne reste jamais verrouillÃ©e ni recouverte, mÃªme si
   le modÃ¨le 3D est lent ou ne se charge pas (rÃ©seau mobile, CDN indisponible).
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
    'use strict';

    function unlockScrollIfStuck() {
        if (document.body.style.overflow === 'hidden') {
            document.body.style.overflow = '';
            document.documentElement.style.overflow = '';
        }
    }

    // L'intro dure ~6 s aprÃ¨s 'loaderFinished' : au-delÃ , la page doit dÃ©filer.
    document.addEventListener('loaderFinished', function () {
        setTimeout(unlockScrollIfStuck, 9000);
    });

    // Dernier recours : si l'intro n'a jamais pu se terminer.
    window.addEventListener('load', function () {
        setTimeout(unlockScrollIfStuck, 30000);
    });

    // Si la scÃ¨ne 3D n'a pas pris la main (Ã©chec de chargement), c'est ici
    // que l'overlay du loader est retirÃ© dÃ¨s que l'on quitte le haut de page.
    window.addEventListener('scroll', function () {
        if (window.__sceneReady) return;
        var loader = document.querySelector('.loader');
        if (!loader) return;
        loader.style.display = window.scrollY > 40 ? 'none' : '';
    }, { passive: true });
})();

/* =====================================================================
   BLOC REECRIT PROPREMENT (corrections de bugs)

/* ---------- 1. Navigation : masque le loader au clic ---------- */
(function () {
    const navLinks = document.querySelectorAll('#main-nav a');
    const loaderElement = document.querySelector('.loader');
    const textContainer = document.querySelector('.text-container');

    navLinks.forEach(link => {
        link.addEventListener('click', () => {
            if (textContainer) {
                textContainer.style.display = 'none';
                textContainer.style.opacity = '0';
            }
            if (loaderElement) loaderElement.style.display = 'none';
            document.body.style.overflow = 'auto';
        });
    });
})();

/* ---------- 2. Apparition progressive des blocs ---------- */
/* Classes concernees (les anciennes .neural-node, .skill-card,
   .domain-card, .cert-node-container ne sont plus dans index.html). */
(function () {
    const targets = document.querySelectorAll(
        '.mission-file, .edu-item, .edu-cta, .see-all-cta, .ref-card, .contact-terminal'
    );
    if (!targets.length) return;

    const observer = new IntersectionObserver((entries, obs) => {
        entries.forEach((entry, i) => {
            if (!entry.isIntersecting) return;
            setTimeout(() => {
                entry.target.style.opacity = '1';
                entry.target.style.transform = 'translateY(0)';
            }, i * 150);
            obs.unobserve(entry.target);
        });
    }, { threshold: 0.3 });

    targets.forEach(el => {
        el.style.opacity = '0';
        el.style.transform = 'translateY(30px)';
        el.style.transition = 'opacity 0.6s ease-out, transform 0.6s cubic-bezier(0.25, 0.46, 0.45, 0.94)';
        observer.observe(el);
    });
})();

/* ---------- 3. Video de fond : pause hors ecran ---------- */
(function () {
    const bgVideo = document.getElementById('bg-video-z1');
    const zone1 = document.getElementById('zone-1');
    if (!bgVideo || !zone1) return;

    new IntersectionObserver(entries => {
        entries.forEach(entry => {
            if (!entry.isIntersecting) bgVideo.pause();
            else bgVideo.play().catch(() => console.log('Lecture auto bloquee par le navigateur'));
        });
    }, { threshold: 0 }).observe(zone1);
})();

/* ---------- 4. Effet 3D sur le terminal de contact ---------- */
(function () {
    const terminal = document.querySelector('.contact-terminal');
    if (!terminal) return;
    const intensity = 5;

    terminal.addEventListener('mousemove', e => {
        const r = terminal.getBoundingClientRect();
        const px = (e.clientX - r.left) / r.width - 0.5;
        const py = (e.clientY - r.top) / r.height - 0.5;
        terminal.style.transform =
            'perspective(1000px) rotateX(' + (py * intensity * -2) + 'deg) rotateY(' + (px * intensity * 2) + 'deg) scale(1.02)';
    });
    terminal.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => { terminal.style.transition = 'none'; });
    terminal.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
        terminal.style.transition = 'transform 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.23, 1, 0.32, 1)';
        terminal.style.transform = 'perspective(1000px) rotateX(0deg) rotateY(0deg) scale(1)';
    });
})();

/* ---------- 5. Boucle : retour en haut depuis le buffer ---------- */
(function () {
    const buffer = document.getElementById('loop-buffer');
    if (!buffer) return;

    new IntersectionObserver(entries => {
        entries.forEach(entry => {
            if (entry.isIntersecting && entry.intersectionRatio > 0.5) {
                document.documentElement.style.scrollSnapType = 'none';
                window.scrollTo(0, 0);
                setTimeout(() => { document.documentElement.style.scrollSnapType = 'y mandatory'; }, 50);
            }
        });
    }, { threshold: 0.6 }).observe(buffer);
})();

/* ---------- 6. Scroll-spy : lien actif dans la nav ---------- */
(function () {
    const links = document.querySelectorAll('#main-nav ul li a');
    const sections = document.querySelectorAll('section[id]');
    if (!links.length || !sections.length) return;

    const spy = new IntersectionObserver(entries => {
        entries.forEach(entry => {
            if (!entry.isIntersecting) return;
            const id = entry.target.getAttribute('id');
            links.forEach(a => {
                a.classList.toggle('active-link', a.getAttribute('href') === '#' + id);
            });
        });
    }, { root: null, rootMargin: '0px', threshold: 0.3 });

    sections.forEach(sec => spy.observe(sec));
})();

/* ---------- 7. Projects : scroller (fleches, barre de progression, glisser) ---------- */
(function () {
    const scroller = document.getElementById('project-scroller');
    const prev = document.getElementById('scroller-prev');
    const next = document.getElementById('scroller-next');
    const bar = document.getElementById('scroller-progress-bar');
    if (!scroller) return;

    function stepSize() {
        const card = scroller.querySelector('.hud-card');
        return card ? card.getBoundingClientRect().width + 15 : 200;
    }

    function update() {
        const max = scroller.scrollWidth - scroller.clientWidth;
        const ratio = max > 0 ? scroller.scrollLeft / max : 0;
        if (prev) prev.disabled = scroller.scrollLeft <= 2;
        if (next) next.disabled = scroller.scrollLeft >= max - 2;
        if (bar && scroller.scrollWidth > 0) {
            const w = Math.min(100, (scroller.clientWidth / scroller.scrollWidth) * 100);
            bar.style.width = w + '%';
            bar.style.transform = 'translateX(' + (ratio * ((100 - w) / w) * 100) + '%)';
        }
    }

    function go(dir) {
        scroller.scrollBy({ left: dir * stepSize() * 2, behavior: 'smooth' });
        if (next) next.classList.add('used');
    }

    /* Largeur du bandeau (desktop) = 4 panels pleins + une zone de fondu.
       Le bouton de dÃ©filement se place donc juste aprÃ¨s le 4e panel. */
    const stripMain = scroller.closest('.scroller-main');
    function sizeStrip() {
        if (!stripMain) return;
        const card = scroller.querySelector('.hud-card');
        if (!card || window.matchMedia('(max-width: 768px)').matches) {
            stripMain.style.removeProperty('--strip-w');
            stripMain.style.removeProperty('--fade-w');
            return;
        }
        const cardW = card.getBoundingClientRect().width + 15;   // largeur + espace entre panels
        const fadeW = Math.round(cardW * 0.9);
        stripMain.style.setProperty('--fade-w', fadeW + 'px');
        stripMain.style.setProperty('--strip-w', Math.round(18 + cardW * 4) + 'px');
        update();
    }
    window.addEventListener('resize', sizeStrip);
    window.addEventListener('load', sizeStrip);
    sizeStrip();

    if (prev) prev.addEventListener('click', () => go(-1));
    if (next) next.addEventListener('click', () => go(1));
    scroller.addEventListener('scroll', update, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener('resize', update);
    update();

    /* Glisser a la souris (le clic sur une carte reste possible) */
    let down = false, startX = 0, startLeft = 0, dragged = false;
    scroller.addEventListener('mousedown', e => {
        down = true; dragged = false;
        startX = e.pageX; startLeft = scroller.scrollLeft;
    });
    window.addEventListener('mousemove', e => {
        if (!down) return;
        const dx = e.pageX - startX;
        if (Math.abs(dx) > 5) dragged = true;
        if (dragged) scroller.scrollLeft = startLeft - dx;
    });
    window.addEventListener('mouseup', () => { down = false; });
    scroller.addEventListener('click', e => {
        if (dragged) { e.stopPropagation(); e.preventDefault(); dragged = false; }
    }, true);
})();

 _0x3ad271.innerText='>> COMMAND_BLOCKED: '+_0x61cb44.key.toUpperCase(),Object.assign(_0x3ad271.style, {
 'position':'fixed','top':'50%','left':'50%','transform':'translate(-50%, -50%)','background':'rgba(10, 15, 20, 0.95)','border':'1px solid #E13C4C','color':'#E13C4C','padding':'10px 20px','fontFamily':'monospace','fontWeight':'bold','zIndex':'10000','boxShadow':'0 0 20px rgba(225, 60, 76, 0.6)'
 }),document.body.appendChild(_0x3ad271),setTimeout(()=>_0x3ad271.remove(),0x320);
 
 }
 
 });

const footer = document.querySelector('.cinematic-footer .copyright');  // le blocage de scroll se fait au niveau du copyright
let footerLocked = false;

if (footer) {
    const footerObserver = new IntersectionObserver(entries => {
        entries.forEach(entry => {
            if (entry.isIntersecting && entry.intersectionRatio > 0.5 && !footerLocked) {
                footerLocked = true;
                document.documentElement.style.overflow = 'hidden';
                setTimeout(() => {
                    document.documentElement.style.overflow = '';
                }, 500);
            } else if (!entry.isIntersecting) {
                footerLocked = false; // on quitte le copyright -> rearme le piege
            }
        });
    }, { threshold: 0.5 });

    footerObserver.observe(footer);
}

/* Affiche les "+" fixes seulement aprÃ¨s la fin du loader */
document.addEventListener('loaderFinished', () => {
    document.body.classList.add('loader-done');
});

/* =====================================================
   More Projects : contrÃ´les manuels (scroll)
   - Cappe la hauteur du marquee Ã  celle de la grille Main
     (afin d'afficher 2 cartes comme la grille en montre 2)
   - Plus de duplication : l'utilisateur scrolle lui-mÃªme
   ===================================================== */
(function () {
    function fitMoreMarquee() {
        const mainGrid    = document.querySelector('.projects-main-grid');
        const moreMarquee = document.querySelector('.projects-more-marquee');
        if (!mainGrid || !moreMarquee) return;

        const mainH = mainGrid.getBoundingClientRect().height;
        if (mainH < 10) return;                        // pas encore rendu

        const isMobile = window.matchMedia('(max-width: 900px)').matches;
        const h = isMobile ? Math.min(mainH, 180) : mainH;
        moreMarquee.style.maxHeight = h + 'px';
    }

    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', fitMoreMarquee);
    } else {
        fitMoreMarquee();
    }
    window.addEventListener('load', fitMoreMarquee);
    window.addEventListener('resize', fitMoreMarquee);

    if (window.ResizeObserver) {
        const mainGrid = document.querySelector('.projects-main-grid');
        if (mainGrid) new ResizeObserver(fitMoreMarquee).observe(mainGrid);
    }
})();


/* =====================================================
   More Projects : capper la hauteur du marquee Ã  celle
   de la grille Main, pour que la rangÃ©e ne soit pas
   Ã©tirÃ©e par le contenu empilÃ© du marquee.
   ===================================================== */
(function () {
    function fitMoreMarquee() {
        const mainGrid    = document.querySelector('.projects-main-grid');
        const moreMarquee = document.querySelector('.projects-more-marquee');
        const morePanel   = document.querySelector('.projects-more');
        if (!mainGrid || !moreMarquee || !morePanel) return;

        // Hauteur rÃ©elle de la grille Main (2 rangÃ©es de cartes)
        const mainH = mainGrid.getBoundingClientRect().height;
        if (mainH < 10) return; // pas encore rendu

        // On cappe le marquee Ã  la hauteur de la grille Main
        // (â‰ˆ 2 hauteurs de carte Main â†’ affiche ~2 cartes "More")
        const isMobile = window.matchMedia('(max-width: 900px)').matches;
        moreMarquee.style.maxHeight = (isMobile ? Math.min(mainH, 180) : mainH) + 'px';

    }

    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', fitMoreMarquee);
    } else {
        fitMoreMarquee();
    }
    window.addEventListener('load', fitMoreMarquee);
    window.addEventListener('resize', fitMoreMarquee);

    // Recalcul dÃ¨s que la grille Main change de taille
    if (window.ResizeObserver) {
        const mainGrid = document.querySelector('.projects-main-grid');
        if (mainGrid) new ResizeObserver(fitMoreMarquee).observe(mainGrid);
    }
})();

        padding: 0;
    }

function calculerSeuils(nbZones) {
  const nbEtapes = nbZones - 1;
  if (nbEtapes <= 0) {
    return { nombreDEtapes: 0, seuils: [] };
  }

  const pas = 1 / nbEtapes;
  const seuils = [];

  for (let i = 1; i < nbEtapes; i++) {
    const valeur = pas * i;
    seuils.push(Number(valeur.toFixed(2)));
  }

  return { nombreDEtapes: nbEtapes, seuils };
}

      hoverWeight: 0
    };

    camera.position.z = zoomDepartIntro;

    if (video) {
      video.play();
    }

    if (window.skipIntroAnimation === true) {
      console.log("Skip-flag dÃ©tectÃ©. Saut de l'animation d'intro.");

      camera.position.z = zoomFinIntro;

      if (hoverAction) {
        hoverAction.weight = 0;
      }

      isIntroAnimationPlaying = false;

      window.scrollTo(0, 0);
      document.body.style.overflow = 'auto';
      document.documentElement.style.overflow = 'auto';

      updateModelOnScroll(0);
    } else {
      console.log("Pas de skip-flag. Lancement de l'animation d'intro.");

      anime({
        targets: etatIntro,
        z: zoomFinIntro,
        duration: 6000,
        easing: 'easeInOutCubic',
        hoverWeight: [
          { value: 1, duration: 3000, easing: 'easeInQuad' },
          { value: 0, duration: 3000, easing: 'easeOutQuad' }
        ],
        update: function () {
          camera.position.z = etatIntro.z;

          if (hoverAction) {
            hoverAction.weight = etatIntro.hoverWeight;
          }
        },
        complete: function () {
          isIntroAnimationPlaying = false;

          window.scrollTo(0, 0);
          document.body.style.overflow = 'auto';
          document.documentElement.style.overflow = 'auto';

          updateModelOnScroll(0);

          const btnNextLoader = document.querySelector('.btn-next-loader');

          if (btnNextLoader) {
            btnNextLoader.classList.add('is-hidden');

            setTimeout(() => {
              btnNextLoader.style.display = 'none';
            }, 400);
          }
        }
      });
    }
  };

  // Le signal est dÃ©jÃ  passÃ© pendant le chargement : on le rejoue maintenant
  if (loaderFinishedFired) onLoaderFinishedHandler();

  mixer = new THREE.AnimationMixer(javelinModel);

  const hoverClip = THREE.AnimationClip.findByName(gltf.animations, 'hover');
  const explodedClip = THREE.AnimationClip.findByName(gltf.animations, 'exploded_view');

  if (hoverClip) {
    hoverAction = mixer.clipAction(hoverClip);
    hoverAction.play();
    hoverAction.weight = 0;
  } else {
    console.error("Animation 'hover' non trouvÃ©e !");
  }

  if (explodedClip) {
    exposedAction = mixer.clipAction(explodedClip);
    exposedAction.play();
    exposedAction.weight = 0;
  } else {
    console.error("Animation 'exploded_view' non trouvÃ©e !");
  }

  window.__sceneReady = true;
}, undefined, (err) => {
  // Ã‰chec de chargement du modÃ¨le (rÃ©seau mobile) : ne jamais laisser la page verrouillÃ©e
  console.error('Chargement du modÃ¨le 3D impossible :', err);
  window.__sceneFailed = true;
  unlockPageScroll();
});

const NB_ZONES = 13;
const configScroll = calculerSeuils(NB_ZONES);

const seuil_Etape1_fin = configScroll.seuils[0];
const seuil_Etape2_fin = configScroll.seuils[1];
const seuil_Etape3_fin = configScroll.seuils[2];
const seuil_Etape4_fin = configScroll.seuils[3];
const seuil_Etape5_fin = configScroll.seuils[4];
const seuil_Etape6_fin = configScroll.seuils[5];
const seuil_Etape7_fin = configScroll.seuils[6];
const seuil_Etape8_fin = configScroll.seuils[7];
const seuil_Etape9_fin = configScroll.seuils[8];
const seuil_Etape10_fin = configScroll.seuils[9];
const seuil_Etape11_fin = configScroll.seuils[10];

const zoomInitial = 77;
const zoomFinal = 300;

const positionY_Initial = -12;
const positionY_Final = -60;

const totalZoomDistance = zoomFinal - zoomInitial;
const zoomStep = totalZoomDistance / 3;

const rotationX_Initial = 0;
const rotationX_Final = Math.PI * 2;

const rotationY_Initial = 0;
const rotationY_Final = Math.PI * 4;

// PrÃ©sent dans la version encryptÃ©e mais non utilisÃ© ensuite.
const seuilAnimation = 0.21;

const unlockThreshold = 0.02;

const htmlEl = document.documentElement;
const loaderText = document.querySelector('.text-container');
const loaderEl = document.querySelector('.loader');

// Hors du haut de page, l'overlay du loader ne doit JAMAIS rester affichÃ©
// (avant : il restait actif si le modÃ¨le n'Ã©tait pas prÃªt ou si un scroll rapide sautait l'Ã©tape 1)
function isAwayFromTop() {
  return window.scrollY > window.innerHeight * 0.15;
}

function syncLoaderWithScroll(scrollPercent) {
  if (scrollPercent >= unlockThreshold || isAwayFromTop()) {
    htmlEl.style.scrollSnapType = 'none';
    if (loaderText) loaderText.style.display = 'none';
    if (loaderEl) {
      loaderEl.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
      loaderEl.style.display = 'none';
    }
  }
}

function updateModelOnScroll(scrollPercent) {
  if (isIntroAnimationPlaying) return;

  syncLoaderWithScroll(scrollPercent);

  if (!javelinModel || !mixer || !hoverAction || !exposedAction) return;

  javelinModel.position.x = 0;

  if (scrollPercent <= seuil_Etape1_fin) {
    javelinModel.visible = true;

    const progress = scrollPercent / seuil_Etape1_fin;
    loaderText.style.opacity = 1 - progress;

    if (scrollPercent >= unlockThreshold || isAwayFromTop()) {
      htmlEl.style.scrollSnapType = 'none';

      if (loaderText) loaderText.style.display = 'none';

      if (loaderEl) {
        loaderEl.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
        loaderEl.style.display = 'none';
      }
    } else {
      htmlEl.style.scrollSnapType = 'y mandatory';

      if (loaderText) loaderText.style.display = 'block';

      if (loaderEl) {
        loaderEl.style.pointerEvents = '';  // le CSS dÃ©cide : le loader ne capte pas les touches, seuls ses boutons le font
        loaderEl.style.display = 'block';
      }
    }

    javelinModel.visible = true;
    camera.position.z = zoomInitial + zoomStep * progress;
    javelinModel.position.y = positionY_Initial;
    javelinModel.rotation.x = rotationX_Initial;
    javelinModel.rotation.y = rotationY_Initial;

    hoverAction.weight = 0;
    exposedAction.weight = 0;

  } else if (scrollPercent <= seuil_Etape2_fin) {
    javelinModel.visible = true;

    const progress = (scrollPercent - seuil_Etape1_fin) /
      (seuil_Etape2_fin - seuil_Etape1_fin);

    camera.position.z = zoomInitial + zoomStep + zoomStep * progress;
    javelinModel.position.y = positionY_Initial;
    javelinModel.rotation.x = rotationX_Initial;
    javelinModel.rotation.y = rotationY_Initial;

    hoverAction.weight = 0;
    exposedAction.weight = 0;

    loaderText.style.display = 'none';

  } else if (scrollPercent <= seuil_Etape3_fin) {
    javelinModel.visible = true;

    const progress = (scrollPercent - seuil_Etape2_fin) /
      (seuil_Etape3_fin - seuil_Etape2_fin);

    camera.position.z = zoomInitial + zoomStep * 2 + zoomStep * progress;
    javelinModel.position.y = positionY_Initial;
    javelinModel.rotation.x = rotationX_Initial;
    javelinModel.rotation.y = rotationY_Initial;

    hoverAction.weight = 0;
    exposedAction.weight = 0;

    loaderText.style.display = 'none';

  } else if (scrollPercent <= seuil_Etape4_fin) {
    javelinModel.visible = true;

    const progress = (scrollPercent - seuil_Etape3_fin) /
      (seuil_Etape4_fin - seuil_Etape3_fin);

    camera.position.z = zoomFinal;
    javelinModel.position.y = positionY_Initial +
      (positionY_Final - positionY_Initial) * progress;

    javelinModel.rotation.x = rotationX_Initial;
    javelinModel.rotation.y = rotationY_Initial;

    hoverAction.weight = 0;
    exposedAction.weight = 0;

    loaderText.style.display = 'none';

  } else if (scrollPercent <= seuil_Etape5_fin) {
    javelinModel.visible = false;

    // Variable prÃ©sente dans la version encryptÃ©e mais non utilisÃ©e.
    const _progressEtape5 = (scrollPercent - seuil_Etape4_fin) /
      (seuil_Etape5_fin - seuil_Etape4_fin);

    camera.position.z = zoomFinal;
    javelinModel.position.y = 300;
    javelinModel.rotation.x = rotationX_Initial;
    javelinModel.rotation.y = rotationY_Initial;

    hoverAction.weight = 0;
    exposedAction.weight = 0;

    loaderText.style.display = 'none';

  } else if (scrollPercent <= seuil_Etape6_fin) {
    javelinModel.visible = false;

    // Variable prÃ©sente dans la version encryptÃ©e mais non utilisÃ©e.
    const _progressEtape6 = (scrollPercent - seuil_Etape5_fin) /
      (seuil_Etape6_fin - seuil_Etape5_fin);

    camera.position.z = zoomFinal;
    javelinModel.position.y = 300;
    javelinModel.rotation.x = rotationX_Initial;
    javelinModel.rotation.y = rotationY_Initial;

    hoverAction.weight = 0;
    exposedAction.weight = 0;

    loaderText.style.display = 'none';

  } else if (scrollPercent <= seuil_Etape7_fin) {
    javelinModel.visible = false;

    // Variable prÃ©sente dans la version encryptÃ©e mais non utilisÃ©e.
    const _progressEtape7 = (scrollPercent - seuil_Etape6_fin) /
      (seuil_Etape7_fin - seuil_Etape6_fin);

    camera.position.z = zoomFinal;
    javelinModel.position.y = 300;
    javelinModel.rotation.x = rotationX_Final;
    javelinModel.rotation.y = rotationY_Final;

    hoverAction.weight = 0;
    exposedAction.weight = 0;

    loaderText.style.display = 'none';

  } else if (scrollPercent <= seuil_Etape8_fin) {
    javelinModel.visible = false;

    // Variable prÃ©sente dans la version encryptÃ©e mais non utilisÃ©e.
    const _progressEtape8 = (scrollPercent - seuil_Etape7_fin) /
      (seuil_Etape8_fin - seuil_Etape7_fin);

    camera.position.z = zoomFinal;
    javelinModel.position.y = 300;
    javelinModel.rotation.x = rotationX_Final;
    javelinModel.rotation.y = rotationY_Final;

    hoverAction.weight = 0;
    exposedAction.weight = 0;

    loaderText.style.display = 'none';

  } else if (scrollPercent <= seuil_Etape9_fin) {
    javelinModel.visible = true;

    const progress = (scrollPercent - seuil_Etape8_fin) /
      (seuil_Etape9_fin - seuil_Etape8_fin);

    hoverAction.weight = 0;
    camera.position.z = zoomFinal;
    javelinModel.position.y = positionY_Final;
    javelinModel.position.x = EDU_DRONE_X;
    exposedAction.weight = progress;

    loaderText.style.display = 'none';

  } else if (scrollPercent <= seuil_Etape10_fin) {
    javelinModel.visible = true;

    const progress = (scrollPercent - seuil_Etape9_fin) /
      (seuil_Etape10_fin - seuil_Etape9_fin);

    hoverAction.weight = 0;
    camera.position.z = zoomFinal;
    javelinModel.position.y = positionY_Final;
    javelinModel.position.x = EDU_DRONE_X;
    exposedAction.weight = 1 - progress;

    loaderText.style.display = 'none';

  } else if (scrollPercent <= seuil_Etape11_fin) {
    javelinModel.visible = true;

    // Variable prÃ©sente dans la version encryptÃ©e mais non utilisÃ©e.
    const _progressEtape11 = (scrollPercent - seuil_Etape10_fin) /
      (seuil_Etape11_fin - seuil_Etape10_fin);

    hoverAction.weight = 0;
    exposedAction.weight = 0;
    javelinModel.position.y = positionY_Final;
    camera.position.z = zoomFinal;

    loaderText.style.display = 'none';

  } else {
    // Variable prÃ©sente dans la version encryptÃ©e mais non utilisÃ©e.
    const _progressFinal = (scrollPercent - seuil_Etape11_fin) /
      (1 - seuil_Etape11_fin);

    hoverAction.weight = 0;
    exposedAction.weight = 0;
    javelinModel.position.y = 300;
    camera.position.z = zoomFinal;

    loaderText.style.display = 'none';
  }
}

let lastKnownScrollPercent = 0;
let ticking = false;

/* =====================================================================
   ANIMATION DU DRONE CALEE SUR LES SECTIONS (et non plus sur le % de scroll)
   ---------------------------------------------------------------------
   updateModelOnScroll() attend toujours une valeur "avancement" entre 0 et 1,
   avec les memes seuils qu'avant (1/12, 2/12 ... 11/12). Cette valeur est
   maintenant calculee a partir de la position des sections : ajouter ou
   retirer une section ne decale donc plus le drone.

   Repere : la ligne au milieu de l'ecran (sauf pour l'intro, au bord haut).
   Chaque ligne du tableau = [position dans la page, avancement associe].
     - Intro + About    : zoom (etapes 1 a 3), puis descente du drone (etape 4)
     - Projects         : drone cache (etapes 5-6)
     - Internship       : drone cache (etapes 7-8)
     - Education        : VUE ECLATEE (etapes 9-10 : 0 -> 1 -> 0 au milieu)
     - References       : drone visible, replie (etape 11)
     - Contact et fin   : drone masque (etape finale)
   ===================================================================== */

// Decalage horizontal optionnel du drone pendant la vue eclatee
// (0 = centre ; valeur positive/negative pour le pousser a droite/gauche
//  si le texte de la section Education le recouvre).
const EDU_DRONE_X = 0;

let droneAnchors = null;

function buildDroneAnchors() {
  const vh = window.innerHeight;
  const sy = window.scrollY;
  const half = vh / 2;

  const el = {
    about: document.getElementById('about'),
    projects: document.getElementById('projects'),
    internship: document.getElementById('internship'),
    education: document.getElementById('education'),
    references: document.getElementById('references'),
    contact: document.getElementById('contact')
  };

  // Si une section manque, on retombe sur l'ancien calcul (pourcentage de scroll)
  if (!Object.values(el).every(Boolean)) return null;

  const edge = (node, which) => {
    const r = node.getBoundingClientRect();
    const y = which === 'bottom' ? r.bottom : which === 'middle' ? (r.top + r.bottom) / 2 : r.top;
    return sy + y;
  };

  const maxScroll = Math.max(1, htmlEl.scrollHeight - vh);

  const raw = [
    [0,                                         0],
    [edge(el.about, 'top'),                     seuil_Etape3_fin],   // fin du zoom
    [edge(el.projects, 'top') - half,           seuil_Etape4_fin],   // fin de la descente
    [edge(el.internship, 'top') - half,         seuil_Etape6_fin],
    [edge(el.education, 'top') - half,          seuil_Etape8_fin],   // debut vue eclatee
    [edge(el.education, 'middle') - half,       seuil_Etape9_fin],   // vue eclatee maximale
    [edge(el.education, 'bottom') - half,       seuil_Etape10_fin],  // retour au drone replie
    [edge(el.references, 'bottom') - half,      seuil_Etape11_fin],
    [maxScroll,                                 1]
  ];

  // Positions strictement croissantes
  const anchors = [];
  raw.forEach(([y, v], i) => {
    const prevY = i === 0 ? -1 : anchors[i - 1][0];
    anchors.push([Math.max(y, prevY + 1), v]);
  });

  return anchors;
}

function computeDroneProgress() {
  if (!droneAnchors) droneAnchors = buildDroneAnchors();

  if (!droneAnchors) {
    // Ancien comportement (secours)
    const max = htmlEl.scrollHeight - window.innerHeight;
    return max > 0 ? window.scrollY / max : 0;
  }

  const y = window.scrollY;
  const a = droneAnchors;

  if (y <= a[0][0]) return a[0][1];

  for (let i = 1; i < a.length; i++) {
    if (y <= a[i][0]) {
      const t = (y - a[i - 1][0]) / (a[i][0] - a[i - 1][0]);
      return a[i - 1][1] + (a[i][1] - a[i - 1][1]) * t;
    }
  }
  return 1;
}

// Les positions changent quand le contenu se charge ou que la fenetre change
if (window.ResizeObserver) {
  new ResizeObserver(() => { droneAnchors = null; }).observe(document.body);
}
window.addEventListener('load', () => { droneAnchors = null; onScroll(); });

function onScroll() {
  lastKnownScrollPercent = computeDroneProgress();

  if (!ticking) {
    window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      updateModelOnScroll(lastKnownScrollPercent);
      ticking = false;
    });

    ticking = true;
  }
}

window.addEventListener('scroll', onScroll);

window.addEventListener('scroll', () => {
  if (window.scrollY < 10 && javelinModel) {
    javelinModel.visible = true;
    updateModelOnScroll(0);
  }
});

function animate() {
  requestAnimationFrame(animate);

  const delta = clock.getDelta();

  if (mixer) {
    mixer.update(delta);
  }

  if (hudNeedsReset) {
    const spinnerWrapper = document.querySelector('.spinner-wrapper');

    if (spinnerWrapper) {
      console.log('animate() : RÃ©initialisation des styles du spinnerWrapper.');
      spinnerWrapper.style.opacity = '1';
      spinnerWrapper.style.filter = 'none';
      spinnerWrapper.style.clipPath = 'none';
    }

    hudNeedsReset = false;
  }

  if (hudObject && javelinModel && !isIntroAnimationPlaying) {
    let rotationY = javelinModel.rotation.y % (Math.PI * 2);

    if (rotationY < 0) {
      rotationY += Math.PI * 2;
    }

    if (rotationY > Math.PI / 2 && rotationY < Math.PI * 3 / 2) {
      hudObject.element.style.visibility = 'hidden';
    } else {
      hudObject.element.style.visibility = 'visible';
    }
  }

  renderer.render(scene, camera);

  if (cssRenderer) {
    cssRenderer.render(scene, camera);
  }
}

animate();

let resizeTimeout;
let lastResizeWidth = document.documentElement.clientWidth;
let lastResizeHeight = window.innerHeight;

window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
  clearTimeout(resizeTimeout);

  resizeTimeout = setTimeout(() => {
    const width = document.documentElement.clientWidth;
    const height = window.innerHeight;

    // Sur mobile, la barre d'adresse qui se masque ne change que la hauteur :
    // inutile (et coÃ»teux) de recrÃ©er le canvas pour quelques pixels.
    if (width === lastResizeWidth && Math.abs(height - lastResizeHeight) < 140) return;
    lastResizeWidth = width;
    lastResizeHeight = height;

    // MÃªme rÃ¨gle que l'initialisation (avant : ratio 2 ou 5 => canvas gÃ©ant, page figÃ©e)
    const ratio = window.innerWidth < 0x300 ? 0.8 : Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 1.2);

    renderer.setSize(width, height);
    renderer.setPixelRatio(ratio);

    if (cssRenderer) {
      cssRenderer.setSize(width, height);
    }

    camera.aspect = width / height;
    camera.updateProjectionMatrix();

    droneAnchors = null;
    onScroll();
  }, 150);
});

--- Fichier : \skills.js ---

/* =====================================================================
   skills.js  -  script de la page skills.html
   1. Onglets des certifications
   2. Bandeaux dÃ©filants (duplication + vitesse)
   3. Lien actif dans la barre de navigation
   ===================================================================== */

/* ---------- 1. ONGLETS ---------- */
(function () {
    const tabs = document.querySelectorAll('.fs-tab');
    if (!tabs.length) return;

    function select(tab) {
        tabs.forEach(t => {
            const active = t === tab;
            t.setAttribute('aria-selected', active ? 'true' : 'false');
            t.tabIndex = active ? 0 : -1;
            const panel = document.getElementById(t.getAttribute('aria-controls'));
            if (panel) panel.hidden = !active;
        });
    }

    tabs.forEach((tab, i) => {
        tab.addEventListener('click', () => select(tab));
        tab.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
            let n = null;
            if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') n = tabs[(i + 1) % tabs.length];
            if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft')  n = tabs[(i - 1 + tabs.length) % tabs.length];
            if (n) { e.preventDefault(); n.focus(); select(n); }
        });
    });
})();

/* ---------- 2. BANDEAUX DÃ‰FILANTS ---------- */
(function () {
    const marquees = document.querySelectorAll('.marquee');
    if (!marquees.length) return;
    
    function setup(m) {
        const track = m.querySelector('.mq-track');
        const first = track.querySelector('.mq-group');
        if (!track || !first) return;

        track.querySelectorAll('.mq-group[data-clone]').forEach(g => g.remove());
        m.classList.remove('is-ready');

        // Vertical si le marquee est dans la section TOOLS
        const isVertical = !!m.closest('#tools');

        const rect = first.getBoundingClientRect();
        const groupSize = isVertical ? rect.height : rect.width;
        if (groupSize < 10) return;

        const containerSize = isVertical ? m.clientHeight : m.clientWidth;
        const copies = Math.ceil(containerSize / groupSize) + 1;
        for (let i = 0; i < copies; i++) {
            const clone = first.cloneNode(true);
            clone.setAttribute('data-clone', '1');
            clone.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
            track.appendChild(clone);
        }

        const speed = parseFloat(m.dataset.speed) || 40;
        m.style.setProperty('--mq-shift', groupSize + 'px');
        m.style.setProperty('--mq-duration', (groupSize / speed) + 's');
        m.classList.add('is-ready');
    }

    function setupAll() { marquees.forEach(setup); }

    // attendre les images (logos) pour mesurer les bonnes largeurs
    window.addEventListener('load', setupAll);
    setupAll();

    let t;
    window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
        clearTimeout(t);
        t = setTimeout(setupAll, 200);
    });
})();

/* ---------- 3. SCROLL-SPY ---------- */
(function () {
    const links = document.querySelectorAll('#sk-nav ul li a');
    const sections = document.querySelectorAll('section[id]');
    if (!links.length || !sections.length) return;

    const spy = new IntersectionObserver(entries => {
        entries.forEach(entry => {
            if (!entry.isIntersecting) return;
            const id = entry.target.id;
            links.forEach(a => a.classList.toggle('active-link', a.getAttribute('href') === '#' + id));
        });
    }, { rootMargin: '-35% 0px -45% 0px', threshold: 0 });

    sections.forEach(s => spy.observe(s));
})();

--- Fichier : \tacticalMap.js ---

// tacticalMap.js - Mise Ã  jour : Collines, Drone Shahed et Viseur fixe

import * as THREE from 'three';

export function initTacticalMap() {
    const canvas = document.getElementById('tactical-map-canvas');
    if (!canvas) return;

    // 1. Initialisation de la scÃ¨ne
    const scene = new THREE.Scene();
    scene.background = new THREE.Color('#0a0a0c'); 
    scene.fog = new THREE.FogExp2('#0a0a0c', 0.015);

    const container = canvas.parentElement;
    const width = container.clientWidth;
    const height = container.clientHeight;
    
    // CamÃ©ra placÃ©e plus haut pour voir la vallÃ©e
    const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(60, width / height, 0.1, 1000);
    camera.position.set(0, 35, 50); 
    camera.lookAt(0, 0, 0);

    let renderer;
    try {
        renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({
            canvas: canvas,
            alpha: true,
            antialias: true,
            powerPreference: 'high-performance'
        });
    } catch (e) {
        console.error('Erreur WebGL :', e);
        canvas.style.display = 'none';
        return;
    }
    renderer.setSize(width, height);
    renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));

    // 2. CrÃ©ation du terrain (Plusieurs collines)
    const terrainGeometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
    const positions = [];
    const colors = [];
    const colorValley = new THREE.Color('#cda47d'); 
    const colorPeak = new THREE.Color('#8b4513');   

    const gridX = 150;
    const gridZ = 150;
    const step = 1.5;

    for (let i = -gridX / 2; i < gridX / 2; i++) {
        for (let j = -gridZ / 2; j < gridZ / 2; j++) {
            if (Math.random() > 0.6) continue; // Points clairsemÃ©s

            const x = i * step;
            const z = j * step;
            
            // Ã‰quation pour crÃ©er plusieurs petites collines et ondulations
            const heightVal = Math.sin(x * 0.2) * Math.cos(z * 0.2) * 5 + Math.sin(x * 0.05) * 8;

            positions.push(x, heightVal, z);

            const mixedColor = colorValley.clone().lerp(colorPeak, (heightVal + 8) / 20);
            colors.push(mixedColor.r, mixedColor.g, mixedColor.b);
        }
    }

    terrainGeometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(positions, 3));
    terrainGeometry.setAttribute('color', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(colors, 3));

    const terrainMaterial = new THREE.PointsMaterial({
        size: 0.3,
        vertexColors: true,
        transparent: true,
        opacity: 2,
        blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending,
        depthWrite: false
    });
    const terrain = new THREE.Points(terrainGeometry, terrainMaterial);
    scene.add(terrain);

    // 3. CrÃ©ation du Drone
    const droneTarget = new THREE.Group();
    scene.add(droneTarget);

    // Forme d'aile delta (type Shahed)
    const droneGeo = new THREE.ConeGeometry(0.8, 1.6, 3);
    droneGeo.rotateX(Math.PI / 2); // On le pointe vers l'avant
    droneGeo.scale(1, 0.15, 1);    // On l'aplatit fortement pour faire une aile
    const droneMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xaaaaaa, wireframe: true });
    const droneMesh = new THREE.Mesh(droneGeo, droneMat);
    droneTarget.add(droneMesh);

    // 4. CrÃ©ation du Viseur (Encadrement)
    // SÃ©parÃ© du drone pour qu'il reste toujours face Ã  la camÃ©ra
    const reticleGroup = new THREE.Group();
    scene.add(reticleGroup); 

    const reticleMat = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: 0xff0000, linewidth: 2 });
    const s = 1.5; // Taille de l'encadrement
    const l = 0.6; // Longueur des bords

    // Fonction pour dessiner de vrais coins " [ ] "
    function createCorner(x, y, scaleX, scaleY) {
        const pts = [
            new THREE.Vector3(x + (l * scaleX), y, 0),
            new THREE.Vector3(x, y, 0),
            new THREE.Vector3(x, y + (l * scaleY), 0)
        ];
        return new THREE.Line(new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(pts), reticleMat);
    }

    reticleGroup.add(createCorner(-s, s, 1, -1));  // Haut-Gauche
    reticleGroup.add(createCorner(s, s, -1, -1));  // Haut-Droite
    reticleGroup.add(createCorner(-s, -s, 1, 1));  // Bas-Gauche
    reticleGroup.add(createCorner(s, -s, -1, 1));  // Bas-Droite

    // 5. Boucle d'Animation
    const clock = new THREE.Clock();

    function animate() {
        if (canvas.classList.contains('paused') || canvas.style.display === 'none') {
            requestAnimationFrame(animate);
            return;
        }
        requestAnimationFrame(animate);

        const t = clock.getElapsedTime();

        // Mouvement du drone (Surface plus large)
        droneTarget.position.x = Math.sin(t * 0.2) * 45;
        droneTarget.position.z = Math.cos(t * 0.25) * 35;
        
        // Calcul du relief sous le drone pour voler en rase-motte (4 unitÃ©s au-dessus du sol)
        const groundY = Math.sin(droneTarget.position.x * 0.2) * Math.cos(droneTarget.position.z * 0.2) * 5 + Math.sin(droneTarget.position.x * 0.05) * 8;
        droneTarget.position.y = groundY + 1.2;

        // Le drone regarde dans la direction oÃ¹ il avance
        const dx = Math.cos(t * 0.2) * 45 * 0.2;
        const dz = -Math.sin(t * 0.25) * 35 * 0.25;
        // On le laisse bien horizontal en gardant son Y actuel pour le lookAt
        droneMesh.lookAt(droneTarget.position.x + dx, droneTarget.position.y, droneTarget.position.z + dz);

        // Le viseur suit la position du drone mais regarde TOUJOURS la camÃ©ra
        reticleGroup.position.copy(droneTarget.position);
        reticleGroup.quaternion.copy(camera.quaternion);

        // Clignotement de l'encadrement : 80% rouge (verrouillÃ©), 20% blanc (recherche)
        const isLocked = (t % 2) < 1.7; // Sur un cycle de 2s, vrai pendant 1.7s
        reticleMat.color.setHex(isLocked ? 0xff0000 : 0xffffff);

        renderer.render(scene, camera);
    }
    animate();

    // 6. Gestion du redimensionnement et optimisation
    window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
        const w = container.clientWidth;
        const h = container.clientHeight;
        camera.aspect = w / h;
        camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
        renderer.setSize(w, h);
    });

    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(entries => {
        entries.forEach(entry => {
            if (!entry.isIntersecting) {
                canvas.classList.add('paused');
            } else {
                canvas.classList.remove('paused');
            }
        });
    });
    observer.observe(canvas);
}

/* =====================================================================
   9. FIABILITÃ‰ TACTILE (index.html)
   ===================================================================== */

/* 1. Le loader plein Ã©cran ne capte plus jamais les touches : seuls ses
      boutons restent cliquables. Avant, si son Ã©tat se dÃ©synchronisait
      (modÃ¨le 3D lent, scroll rapide...), une couche invisible couvrait
      toute la page et "mangeait" tous les clics. */
.loader { pointer-events: none; }
.loader a,
.loader button,
.btn-next-loader { pointer-events: auto; }
.btn-next-loader.is-hidden { pointer-events: none; }

/* 2. Hauteur rÃ©ellement visible sur mobile (100vh dÃ©passe sous la barre
      d'adresse : boutons du bas masquÃ©s ou hors d'atteinte du pouce). */
@supports (height: 100dvh) {
    .loader { height: 100dvh; }
}
.btn-next-loader {
    bottom: max(40px, calc(env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 0px) + 24px));
    right: max(40px, calc(env(safe-area-inset-right, 0px) + 24px));
}

/* 3. Gestes tactiles : pas de dÃ©lai de 300 ms ni de zoom au double-tap,
      retour visuel au toucher. */
a, button, .btn-icon, .btn-tube, .btn-sober, .btn-filter,
.hud-card, .hud-card-mini, .footer-link, #main-nav a {
    touch-action: manipulation;
    -webkit-tap-highlight-color: rgba(225, 60, 76, 0.25);
}

/* 4. Cibles tactiles d'au moins 44 px (recommandation Apple / WCAG). */
@media (pointer: coarse) {
    .btn-icon, .btn-tube, .btn-sober, .btn-filter {
        min-height: 44px;
        min-width: 44px;
    }
    #main-nav a, .main-nav li a {
        display: inline-flex;
        align-items: center;
        min-height: 44px;
    }
}

/* 5. Pas d'effet de survol "collant" aprÃ¨s un toucher. */
@media (hover: none) {
    .hud-card-mini:hover { transform: none; }
    .btn-icon.btn-linkedin:hover { transform: none; }
}
